          <p>(c) The referendum revenue authorization for the newly created district shall continue for a period of time equal to the longest period authorized for any component district. The referendum revenue authorization for the newly created district may be modified pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17#stat.126C.17.9">126C.17, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Discontinued referendum revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>If the plan for consolidation provides for discontinuance of referendum revenue previously approved by voters of the component districts pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17#stat.126C.17.9">126C.17, subdivision 9</a>, or its predecessor provision, the newly created district must not receive referendum revenue unless the voters of the newly created district authorize referendum revenue pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.17#stat.126C.17.9">126C.17, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.54.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Taxable property.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">As of the effective date of a consolidation of districts or the dissolution of a district and its attachment to one or more existing districts pursuant to chapter 123A, all the taxable property which is in the newly created or enlarged district and which was previously taxable for the payment of any statutory operating debt theretofore incurred by any preexisting district of which the taxable property was a part prior to the consolidation or dissolution and attachment shall remain taxable for the payment of that debt and shall not become taxable for the payment of any statutory operating debt theretofore incurred by any preexisting district of which the taxable property was not a part prior to the consolidation or dissolution and attachment. The amount of statutory operating debt attributable to that taxable property and to the newly created or enlarged district in which it is located, and the amount of a preexisting district's reserved fund balance reserve account for purposes of statutory operating debt reduction attributable to the newly created or enlarged district, shall be apportioned according to the proportion which the adjusted net tax capacity of that part of the preexisting district bears to the total adjusted net tax capacity of the entire preexisting district at the time of the consolidation or dissolution and attachment. This apportionment shall be made by the county auditor and shall be incorporated as an annex to the order of the commissioner dividing the assets and liabilities of the component districts. As used in this section, "statutory operating debt" shall have the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.81">123B.81</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Reorganization operating debt levies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A district that receives revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.39#stat.123A.39.3">123A.39, subdivision 3</a>, for cooperation or has combined according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a> may levy to eliminate reorganization operating debt as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.82">123B.82</a>, clause (1). The amount of the debt must be certified over a period of five years. After the effective date of combination according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a>, the levy may be certified and spread either</p>
          <p>(1) only on the property in the combined district that would have been taxable in the preexisting district that incurred the debt, or</p>
          <p>(2) on all of the taxable property in the combined district.</p>
          <p>(b) A district that has reorganized according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48">123A.48</a> may levy to eliminate reorganization operating debt as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.82">123B.82</a>, clause (2). The amount of debt must be certified over a period not to exceed five years and may be spread either</p>
          <p>(1) only on the property in the newly created or enlarged district which was taxable in the preexisting district that incurred the debt, or</p>
          <p>(2) on all of the taxable property in the newly created or enlarged district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.54.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.54.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 54</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.73.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Levy for severance pay or early retirement incentives.</span></h2>
          <p>The board of a newly created or enlarged district to which part or all of a dissolved district was attached according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a> may levy for severance pay or early retirement incentives for licensed and nonlicensed employees who resign or retire early as a result of the dissolution or consolidation, if the commissioner approves the incentives and the amount to be levied. The amount may be levied over a period of up to five years and must be spread in whole or in part on the property of a preexisting district or the newly created or enlarged district, as determined by the board of the newly created or enlarged district.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.73.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/764/#laws.0.26.0">1978 c 764 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/50/#laws.0.12.0">1979 c 50 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/509/#laws.0.31.0">1980 c 509 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.1.1.0">1980 c 609 art 1 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.1.3.0">1981 c 358 art 1 s 3</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.1.22.0">1983 c 314 art 1 s 22</a>; art 6 s 4; art 7 s 11-14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/248/#laws.0.31.0">1985 c 248 s 31</a>, 32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.1.1.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 1 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.7.42.0">1987 c 398 art 7 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/486/#laws.0.13.0">1988 c 486 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.84.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.1.3.0">1991 c 265 art 1 s 3</a>; art 6 s 16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.1.3.0">1992 c 499 art 1 s 3</a>-5; art 6 s 11-13; art 12 s 6,7,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.6.8.0">1994 c 647 art 6 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/212/#laws.4.64.0">1995 c 212 art 4 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.58.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 58</a>-60,104; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.1.3.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 1 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.11.2.0">2007 c 146 art 11 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.1.3.0">2009 c 96 art 1 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.1.3.0">2013 c 116 art 1 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.5.2.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 5 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.74">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.74 OBLIGATIONS UPON DISTRICT REORGANIZATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.74.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Capital loan obligations.</span></h2>
          <p>If a district has a capital loan outstanding at the time of reorganization according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48">123A.48</a>, or sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a>, and if the plan for reorganization provides for payment of all or a portion of the capital loan obligation by the newly created or enlarged district or makes no provision for payment, all of the taxable property in the newly created or enlarged district is taxable for the payment to the extent stated in the plan. Notwithstanding any contract to the contrary, if all of the taxable property in the newly created or enlarged district is taxable for the payment of the capital loan and until the capital loan is retired or canceled, the maximum effort debt service levy must be recalculated annually by the department to be equal to the required debt service levy plus an additional amount. The additional amount must be the greater of:</p>
          <p>(i) zero, or</p>
          <p>(ii) the maximum effort debt service levy of the preexisting district minus the required debt service levy of the preexisting district that received the capital loan.</p>
          <p>For the purpose of the recalculation, additional bond issues after the date of the reorganization shall not impact the maximum effort debt service levy or the required debt service levy.</p>
          <p>Notwithstanding any contract to the contrary, the plan for reorganization may specify that the obligation for a capital loan remains solely with the preexisting district that incurred the obligation. This subdivision does not relieve any property from any tax liability for payment of any capital loan obligation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.74.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Energy loan obligations.</span></h2>
          <p>If a district has an energy loan outstanding at the time of reorganization according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.48">123A.48</a>, or sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.35">123A.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.43">123A.43</a>, and if the plan for reorganization provides for payment of all or a portion of the energy loan obligation by the newly created or enlarged district or makes no provision for payment, all of the taxable property in the newly created or enlarged district is taxable for the payment.</p>
          <p>Notwithstanding any contract to the contrary, the plan for reorganization may specify that the obligation for an energy loan remains solely with the preexisting district that incurred the obligation. This subdivision does not relieve any property from any tax liability for payment of any energy loan obligation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.74.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.6.17.0">1991 c 265 art 6 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.61.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 61</a>,104; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.75">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.75 EMPLOYEES OF REORGANIZED DISTRICTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.75.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Teacher assignment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As of the effective date of a consolidation in which a district is divided or the dissolution of a district and its attachment to two or more existing districts, each teacher employed by an affected district shall be assigned to the newly created or enlarged district on the basis of a ratio of the pupils assigned to each district according to the new district boundaries. The district receiving the greatest number of pupils must be assigned the teacher with the greatest seniority, and the remaining teachers must be alternately assigned to each district until the district receiving the fewest pupils has received its ratio of teachers who will not be retiring before the effective date of the consolidation or dissolution.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), the board and the exclusive representative of teachers in each district involved in the consolidation or dissolution and attachment may negotiate a plan for assigning teachers to each newly created or enlarged district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.75.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Collective bargaining.</span></h2>
          <p>The organization certified as the exclusive bargaining representative for the teachers in the particular preexisting district which employed the largest proportion of the teachers who are assigned to a new employing district according to subdivision 1 shall be certified as the exclusive bargaining representative for the teachers assigned to that new employing district, until that organization is decertified or another organization is certified in its place pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.01">179A.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.25">179A.25</a>. For purposes of negotiation of a new contract with the board of the new employing district and the certification of an exclusive bargaining representative for purposes of that negotiation, the teachers assigned to that district shall be considered an appropriate unit of employees of that district as of the date the county board orders its interlocutory order of dissolution and attachment to be final and effective or as of the date the commissioner assigns an identification number to a new district created by consolidation. During the school year before the consolidation becomes effective, the newly elected board or the board of the district to which a dissolved district is attached, may place teachers assigned to it on unrequested leave of absence as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40">122A.40</a> according to: (a) a plan negotiated in a new master contract between it and the exclusive bargaining representative of the teachers assigned to it, or (b) if no such plan exists, an applicable plan negotiated in the contract which according to this subdivision will temporarily govern the terms and conditions of employment of teachers assigned to it.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.75.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Interim contractual agreements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Until a successor contract is executed between the new board and the exclusive representative of the teachers of the new district, the boards of both districts and the exclusive representatives of the teachers of both districts may agree:</p>
          <p>(1) to comply with the contract of either district with respect to all of the teachers assigned to the new district; or</p>
          <p>(2) that each of the contracts shall apply to the teachers previously subject to the respective contract.</p>
          <p>(b) In the absence of an agreement according to paragraph (a), the following shall apply:</p>
          <p>(1) if the effective date is July 1 of an even-numbered year, each of the contracts shall apply to the teachers previously subject to the respective contract and shall be binding on the new board; or</p>
          <p>(2) if the effective date is July 1 of an odd-numbered year, the contract of the district that previously employed the largest proportion of teachers assigned to the new district applies to all of the teachers assigned to the new district and shall be binding on the new board. The application of this section shall not result in a reduction in a teacher's basic salary, payments for cocurricular or extracurricular assignments, district contributions toward insurance coverages or tax-sheltered annuities, leaves of absence, or severance pay until a successor contract is executed between the new board and the exclusive representative.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.75.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Contracts; termination; tenure.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in this section, the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40">122A.40</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41</a> shall apply to the employment of each teacher by the new employing district on the same basis as they would have applied to the employment if the teacher had been employed by that new district before the effective date of the consolidation or dissolution and attachment.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.75.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/764/#laws.0.27.0">1978 c 764 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/4/#laws.1.46.0">1Sp1981 c 4 art 1 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/462/#laws.0.27.0">1984 c 462 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.6.15.0">1989 c 329 art 6 s 15</a>-17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.6.14.0">1992 c 499 art 6 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.1.5.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.62.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 62</a>-64,104; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.28.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 28</a>,29</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.76">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.76 EXPENSES OF TRANSITION.</h1>
        <p>The board of a district to which a dissolved district is attached pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.46">123A.46</a>, may, for the purpose of paying the expenses of negotiations and other administrative expenses relating to the transition, enter into agreements with banks or any person to take its orders at any rate of interest not to exceed seven percent per annum. These orders shall be paid by the treasurer of the district from district funds after the effective date of the dissolution and attachment. Notwithstanding the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.22">124D.22</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.40">126C.40</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.45">126C.45</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.48">126C.48</a>, the district may, in the year the dissolution and attachment becomes effective, levy an amount equal to the amount of the orders issued pursuant to this subdivision and the interest on these orders. No district shall issue orders for funds or make a levy pursuant to this subdivision without the commissioner's approval of the expenses to be paid with the funds from the orders and levy.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.76.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/764/#laws.0.28.0">1978 c 764 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.12.29.0">1992 c 499 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.6.9.0">1994 c 647 art 6 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/212/#laws.4.64.0">1995 c 212 art 4 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.104.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 104</a>; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.77">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.77 SURPLUS COUNTY SCHOOL TAX FUNDS; DISTRIBUTION.</h1>
        <p>When, by reason of reorganization of districts, there is a surplus in the county treasury to the credit of the county school tax fund on account of an excessive tax levy already made, and when there is no need for the surplus, the county treasurer shall pay the surplus to the reorganized district upon the order of the county board.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.77.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/157/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 157 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.99.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 99</a>,104</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.78">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.78 JOINT POWERS AGREEMENTS FOR FACILITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.78.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Instructional facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>Any group of districts may form a joint powers district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a> representing all participating districts to build or acquire a facility to be used for instructional purposes. The joint powers board must submit the project for review and comment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71">123B.71</a>. The joint powers board must hold a hearing on the proposal. The joint powers district must submit the question of authorizing the borrowing of funds for the project to the voters of the joint powers district at a special election. The question submitted shall state the total amount of funding needed from all sources. The joint powers board may issue the bonds according to chapter 475 and certify the levy required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.61">475.61</a> only if a majority of those voting on the question vote in the affirmative and only after the school boards of each member district have adopted a resolution pledging the full faith and credit of that district. The resolution shall irrevocably commit that district to pay a proportionate share, based on pupil units, of any debt levy shortages that, together with other funds available, would allow the joint powers board to pay the principal and interest on the obligations. The district's payment of its proportionate share of the shortfall shall be made from the district's capital expenditure fund. The clerk of the joint powers board must certify the vote of the bond election to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.123A.78.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Shared facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>A group of governmental units may form a joint powers district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a> representing all participating units to build or acquire a facility. The joint powers board must submit the project for review and comment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.71">123B.71</a>. The joint powers board must hold a hearing on the proposal. The joint powers district must submit the question of authorizing the borrowing of funds for the project to the voters of the joint powers district at a special election. The question submitted shall state the total amount of funding needed from all sources. The joint powers board may issue the bonds according to chapter 475 and certify the levy required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/475.61">475.61</a> only if a majority of those voting on the question vote in the affirmative and only after the boards of each member unit have adopted a resolution pledging the full faith and credit of that unit. The resolution must irrevocably commit that unit to pay an agreed-upon share of any debt levy shortages that, together with other funds available, would allow the joint powers board to pay the principal and interest on the obligations. The clerk of the joint powers board must certify the vote of the bond election to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.78.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.11.1.0">1990 c 562 art 11 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.5.4.0">1991 c 265 art 5 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.1.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 1</a>,104; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.123A.79">
        <h1 class="shn">123A.79 MEETINGS OF JOINT POWERS BOARD.</h1>
        <p>(a) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, a joint powers board established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.443">123A.443</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.78">123A.78</a>, and the board of each of its member districts may hold meetings at a facility operated by the joint powers board.</p>
        <p>(b) The joint powers board shall establish and maintain a schedule of the time and place of its meetings and shall give notice of regular and special meetings as required under chapter 13D.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.123A.79.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.6.7.0">1990 c 562 art 6 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.5.104.0">1998 c 397 art 5 s 104</a>; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
    </div>
